B: Kw' ot naai haai i.
6. A: Naaj naai haai chi？
B: Nang naai haaf chi？
7.A: Naad naai haai hot*？
B: Kw' ot naai haaf hog*.
8. A: Kw'ot yit naai haai mot？
B: Nang yit naai haai chi.
9. A: Naat yit naai haai wa-po*？
B: Kw'ot yit naai haai wa-po*.

1.A: Ho 1a ma？

2. As Kw' ot chi haaf m-haai foon-pit？
B： Haai,kw' og chi haai foon-pit.
3. A: Ning chi haai m-haai yon-pit？
B： M-haai, ngng chi m-haai yon-pit？
4. A: kw'o yit-poo haai m-haai t' o-kn poa*？

5. A: Ngng yit chiang haai m-haai po-chi？

6. A: Kw' os pon haai to6-ing m-haar？
B: Haai, ngng pon haai too-ing.
7. A: Ning chi haai foon-pit m-haai？
B: M-haaf, kw'ot chi m-haat foon-pit.
8. A: Kw'ot yit-chi haai yon-pit m-haar？

ORAL MATERIAL

今日係禮
拜幾？
你.程拜
四，即係星期吗.
如恩今日係禮拜凹，聽早係禮拜线？
如兴今日係禮拜四，聽早係禮拜五，又喊做星期五。
如果聽早係禮拜五，後日係禮拜幾？
如果聽早係禮拜五，後日係禮拜六，或者星期六。
如果後日係禮拜六，大後日係禮拜幾？
如果後日係禮拜六，大後日係禮拜日，即係
禮拜
，或者星期日.
如果今日係星期四，昨日係星期幾？
如果合日係星期四，昨日係星期三，又喊做禮拜三.
如果啱日係星期三，前日係星期幾？
如果啱日係星期三，前日係星期二，或者禮拜二.
如果前日係星期二，大前日係星期幾？
如果前日係星期二，大前日係星期一

POLICE

Do cases of robbery,rape,larceny,murder,manslaughter，
and suicide occur in every city？
Cheung: Cases of robbery,rape,larceny,murder,manslaughter，
and suicide occur in every city. Cities of south China
are no exception. But the internal control and regula-
tion of cities in south China is considered good.
W：
What organization is charged with the maintenance of internal
order in each commonity？
C：
Each community has the Police Department to maintain internal
order.
w：
what method does the Police Department of each community use
to maintain interna1 order？
C：
Every city has a police department and within each district
of the city there is a police station.
W：
what is the primary mission of the police force？
C: The primary mission of the police force is to maintain 1oca1
internal order, to protect lives and properties of the citi-
zens, to prevent commission of crimes,and to maintain traf-
fic order. But their wartime mis sion is to prevent spy ac-
tivities, to assist in air defense,evacuate the population，
etc.
